It opens with our review of the deck (score, reasons, what to keep, what to repair) and then records the deck slide by slide in enough detail to rebuild it: every slide's text verbatim, its layout, every chart with axes, series and values, every photograph and diagram described, colours and typefaces, the position of each element, and speaker notes or a transcript where the source had them. ## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a consistent, recognisable identity (grey rounded panels, bold sans titles, the eye-can product imagery) and a mostly logical route from problem (slide 2) to traction (4), team (5-7), product (8-11), campaigns, retail, projections, round terms (20-22) and exit (25). Many slides are well built, with strong product and campaign photography (13, 14), clear stat cards (4, 6) and a readable pricing grid (34). The weaknesses are consequential: the investment case is split by 14 pages of campaign, review and retail material, the funding ask is not stated until slide 20, and several slides carry very small text (3, 33, 39, 21) that is hard to read in a pitch. The market map on slide 35 has an empty quadrant, uneven product placement and axis labels that sit awkwardly, and the thank-you and appendix repeat slides (26, 42, 28, 8).

**Worth keeping:** Keep the consistent panel and title system, the large-number stat cards on slides 4 and 6, the Breaking Bad and Miami Vice campaign photo spreads on 13 and 14, and the pricing grid on 34.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge or cut the micro-text on slides 33 and 39, make the market map on 35 readable with clear axis placement and a labelled claim, and remove duplicate slides (26 and 42, 28 and 8).

**What would change the score:** I would move the funding ask and use of funds earlier, compress the campaign and review slides (12-15, 40-41) into fewer pages, and give each data slide a headline that states its takeaway.

_Confidence medium. 42 of 42 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a private and confidential investment proposal dated January 2026, then frames a gap in the market for focus-enhancing products beyond caffeine. It introduces Neutonic as a nootropic brand and presents its traction: cases sold, revenue since November 2023, subscriber numbers and channel and location splits. Next come the founding team and creator partners, the product lineup and key ingredient blend, and a 2026 launch plan with a product roadmap. Several slides show launch campaigns, customer reviews, and margin and cost improvements. The deck then covers UK and USA retail rollout progress, revenue projections, the funding round terms with an illustrative investor return table, a valuation rationale based on revenue multiples, investment objectives, and market growth data. It closes with a future-acquisition section naming possible buyers and recent deals, followed by thank-you and appendix slides with product details, pricing, the clinical science behind each ingredient, a market map, and customer profiles. Several slides are worth studying for how they are built, including the product comparison map and the ingredient science slides.

## Questions about this deck

**How many pages does the Neutonic investment deck have?**

The Neutonic deck has 42 pages according to its page count. The slide text provided runs through slide 29, and the deck closes with a thank-you slide and appendix slides.

**What does the Neutonic deck say about the size and growth of the nootropic market?**

Slide 24 says the nootropic market is set to grow from $14bn to $24bn in 7 years. It also shows the market at $14bn in 2023 and $24bn in 2030.

**What funding round terms does the Neutonic deck give?**

Slide 20 says Neutonic is raising $3 million at a $60 million valuation, with $1.25 million already committed. The remaining $1.75 million is being opened to a small group of strategic partners.

**What product types does Neutonic's product lineup include according to the deck?**

Slide 8 lists four product types: Productivity Drink, Brain Capsules, Focus Blends and Creatine Sticks. Each is described as having a different use case and potential customer.

Introducing Neutonic
Company Overview
neutonic

**Layout**

Two-part layout. Left column (about 4% to 40% of width) holds the bold title and subtitle at top, a large neutonic wordmark below, two left-aligned body paragraphs, and a small pixelated neutonic logo at bottom left. **Visuals**

Large black 'neutonic' wordmark in a heavy high-contrast serif display face at left, about 29% wide. At bottom left is a small, low-resolution pixelated copy of the neutonic logo on a slightly lighter checkered patch. On the right is a 3D render of five tall slim cans in a row, descending left to right along the sloped edge of a light grey stepped block. The cans are yellow with a pink band, white with a teal eye, lime green, light blue with a red eye, and orange with a yellow-amber eye. Each can carries a neutonic logo, the words 'PRODUCTIVITY DRINK', a psychedelic swirl of pink, yellow, blue and white around a large eye with a coloured iris, 'FUEL YOUR FOCUS', ingredient text, a flavour label and 'FOOD SUPPLEMENT 330ML'. Lighting is soft and even, with diagonal soft shadows on the grey block. The background is pale grey and the image is full-bleed at right and bottom. A flat #D9D9D9 rectangle fills the left part of the step block.

**Text**

**Layout**

Left column (about 5% to 48% wide) holds a left-aligned title, subtitle and intro paragraph, then two full-width white rounded cards stacked, then two half-width cards side by side. The right half holds a cut-out photo of two men, with two white pill labels at the bottom. No visible footer or page number. A blurred pixelated patch sits at bottom-left.

**Visuals**

Cut-out photograph on the right (about 46% to 100% wide, 4% to 100% tall) of two smiling, bearded, short-haired muscular men in black tops, shown from the thighs up. The man on the left (Chris) wears a black short-sleeve t-shirt, drapes his arm over the shoulder of the man on the right (James), who wears a black sleeveless shirt. Chris holds two yellow-orange cans with an eye illustration (neutonic branding). Bright, even studio lighting, with the background removed and the figures on the light grey slide. Two white pill labels with a thin navy outline sit over their torsos at the bottom. Five white rounded-corner cards sit on the left. A pixelated, blurred grey mosaic at bottom-left (about 3.7% to 19% wide, 85% to 95% tall) obscures a logo, and a faint pixelated lighter border runs around the slide edges.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #E9E9EB with a pixelated, lighter blurred border fade. Light grey background with soft pixelated edge fade. Title is near-black charcoal #221F20, bold geometric sans-serif (Kumbh Sans Bold), about 5.4% of slide height. Subtitle and intro are regular weight, #221F20, 3.2% and 2.7%. Cards are white #FFFFFF with large corner radii (about 2% of slide height), no shadow. Card headings are bold black uppercase at 3.5% (names) or 2.7% (Luke and Shan). Big stat numbers are bold black at 5.7%, with regular black 2.2% captions centered beneath. Pill labels are white with a navy outline, bold black uppercase text.

**Visuals**

Circular photo of Chris Williamson, a man with short light brown hair and stubble, looking up and to the side, in a white t-shirt against a bright teal-blue background. Social icons in a row: red YouTube play button, blue Facebook logo, black TikTok circle logo, Instagram gradient logo, black X circle logo, outline envelope icon. A red YouTube logo in the YouTube card. A line icon of three people in the demographics card. Thin light grey divider lines separate the stats in the YouTube card and the demographics card. At right is a portrait image of a '2025 Top 10 Podcasts Globally' graphic: black textured background, yellow and black striped 2025 numerals at top, white title text, and a 2-column list of ten podcast cover thumbnails with ranks and names. The Modern Wisdom entry (8) is outlined with a red rectangle.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EDEDED pixelated mosaic pattern. Light grey #EDEDED background with a pixelated grey block pattern, especially at the bottom left. White cards #FFFFFF with large rounded corners and no visible shadow. Black text #000000, title #221F20, in Kumbh Sans (geometric sans-serif), bold for numbers and headings and regular for labels. Name about 4.4% of slide height, big numbers about 5.2% and 3.95%, headings 2.5%, labels 1.85%. Red highlight outline #E02020.

**Layout**

Dashboard-style grid of white rounded cards on a light grey background. Title name top left above a circular portrait that overlaps a tall bio card at left. A wide top card holds five social icon stats, a smaller Live Shows card sits at top right, and below are four tall stat cards (YouTube, Audience Demographics, Instagram, Print) in a row. All content is center-aligned within its card. No footer or page number.

**Visuals**

Circular photo portrait of a bearded man with short tousled light-brown hair, serious expression, wearing a dark t-shirt, framed head and shoulders against a turquoise/mint (about #6FDCCB) circle background, overlapping the top edge of the bio card. Icons: red YouTube play button logo (top card and YouTube card), blue Facebook logo circle, black TikTok circle logo, Instagram gradient (purple-orange-yellow) logo in top card and Instagram card, black outline envelope icon (email), outlined group-of-three-people icon (audience demographics), open book illustration with blue cover and line-drawn pages (print). Thin light grey horizontal divider lines separate stat groups within the YouTube, Demographics, Instagram and Print cards.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EEEEEE with a subtle pixelated mosaic of lighter and darker grey squares. Light grey background with faint pixelated mosaic of lighter and slightly darker grey squares. White cards with large rounded corners (about 2% of slide height radius) and no visible shadow. Typeface Kumbh Sans (geometric sans-serif): bold black headings and big numbers, regular weight for labels and body. Title near-black #221F20 bold about 4.4% of slide height; big stats about 5.2% bold, top-card stats about 4% bold; card headings uppercase bold about 2.5%; labels about 1.85%. Bio text 2.5% centered with key phrases in bold. Text colour black #000000, with no coloured accents beyond icon colours and the turquoise portrait circle.

### Slide 8

**Text**

Product Lineup
Since launching with our Productivity Drinks in 2023, we have significantly expanded and now have four amazing
products types, each has a different use case and potential customer.
PRODUCTIVITY DRINK
Our Original, Powerful, and
Delicious Caffeinated Blend
BRAIN CAPSULES
Our Ultimate Blend,
in Daily Pill Form
FOCUS BLENDS
Our Uncaffeinated Stick Packs,
Perfect For Anytime Of Day
CREATINE STICKS
Our Creapure® Creatine Sticks,
Designed to Fuel Your Performance

**Layout**

Title and two-line intro paragraph left-aligned at the top with about 4% left margin. Below, four equal columns, each with a centered bold uppercase heading, a two-line centered description, and a product image beneath. No footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Four product photo renders on a transparent/light background, one per column. Column 1: a row of five tall slim beverage cans (blue, pink, white at center and front, orange, lime green), each printed with the neutonic wordmark and a psychedelic iridescent eye illustration (pink, cyan, orange and green irises), with 'FUEL YOUR FOCUS' text; the cans overlap, with the white one in front. Column 2: a dark black-capped supplement bottle with a white-to-pastel label showing the neutonic wordmark, an iridescent eye with a blue iris and the text 'BRAIN CAPSULES', tilted slightly and surrounded by about eight floating dark red capsules, some motion-blurred. Column 3: yellow and pink neutonic Focus Blend boxes, open at the top with stick packs, plus two upright stick sachets (orange-yellow and pink) at the left, with soft shadows beneath. Column 4: three tall white and silver creatine sachets with yellow, blue and red-pink eyes standing behind three white neutonic Creatine Sachets boxes (red, white center and yellow base strips) with eye illustrations. The lighting is soft studio light. All images are uncropped and sit directly on the background with no frames.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EDEDED with a subtle pattern of slightly lighter and darker rectangular tiles. Light grey background with faint lighter square tiles. Title in near-black #221F20 Kumbh Sans Bold, about 5.4% of slide height. Intro text is black Kumbh Sans Regular, about 3% of height. Column headings are bold uppercase Kumbh Sans in #221F20, about 3.2% of height. Descriptions are regular Kumbh Sans in #1D1C1D, about 2.5% of height. The products supply the colour accents (yellow, pink, cyan, orange). There are no lines, shapes or shadows apart from those in the product renders.

### Slide 9

**Text**

Our Product Lineup
Our Key Nootropic Blend
Our formulation is crafted with carefully / selected ingredients, prioritizing proven / effectiveness and exceptional quality and / we only use:
- Optimal dosages of each ingredient / for maximum impact.
- Human-tested ingredients to ensure / safety and efficacy.
- Ingredients with studies that are / based on our demographic.
Cognizin
Cognizin® is a trusted nootropic. / Studies suggest it can enhance cognitive / function and mental clarity.
L-Theanine
L-theanine is a natural amino acid with studies / that link it to composure and the calm mental / state associated with alpha brain wave activity.
Panax Ginseng
Evidence suggests that Panax / ginseng can aid cognitive / function over a sustained period.
Rhodiola Rosea
Rhodiola rosea is a powerful / adaptogen. Research suggests that it / aids the body in alleviating the fatigue / associated with psychological stress.
Caffeine
Caffeine is a natural stimulant that increases / alertness and is used worldwide to reduce the / feeling of fatigue.
B Vitamins
B vitamins are vital to avoid compounding / cognitive decline. A natural source of vitality / and focus.

**Layout**

Left column holds the title, subtitle, intro paragraph and a three-item dash bullet list, left-aligned with a margin of about 4% of slide width. The right side has a staggered grid of six white rounded cards in three rows of two, with the middle row offset outward. A circular eye graphic overlaps the centre of the grid. No footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Six white rounded-corner cards, each with a product image, a serif name and a centred sans-serif description. Card 1 (top left): blue Cognizin wordmark logo with a red dotted arc above the 'i' and a small ® mark. Card 2 (top right): photo of fresh green tea leaves beside a small pile of dried dark tea leaves, on white. Card 3 (middle left): photo of a pale tan ginseng root with thin hair roots, lying horizontally. Card 4 (middle right): photo of a green Rhodiola rosea plant with yellow-orange flower clusters. Card 5 (bottom left): photo of roasted brown coffee beans in a cluster. Card 6 (bottom right): photo of a heap of colourful fresh fruit and vegetables (pineapple, tomatoes, peppers, bananas, oranges, greens). At the centre of the grid, overlapping the four inner card corners, is a circular 3D-style iridescent sphere with a stylised eye: white almond-shaped eye, pale green iris, black pupil with a small white highlight, wrapped in swirls of pink, yellow, orange, purple, blue and teal.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EEEEEE with a faint pattern of lighter and darker grey squares (tiled image covering the slide). Light grey background made of faint square tiles in slightly different greys. Title is near-black charcoal #221F20 in bold geometric sans-serif (Kumbh Sans Bold), about 5.4% of slide height. Subtitle is Kumbh Sans Regular #221F20, about 3.2%. Body and bullets are Kumbh Sans Light in black #000000, about 3% of slide height, with key phrases in Kumbh Sans Bold. Card titles are Libre Caslon Text Bold #221F20, about 2%, centred. Card descriptions are Kumbh Sans Regular #221F20, about 1.5%, centred. Cards are pure white #FFFFFF with large corner radii (about 2% of slide width) and no visible shadow. The eye sphere has a glossy, saturated gradient finish.

### Slide 10

**Text**

Product Detail
Key Nootropic Benefits
Unlock your potential with our / powerful nootropic blend / designed to make you feel / less stressed, less fatigued and / genuinely more focused.
Improved Attention
Feel more motivated, / uplifted and better able / to get things done.
Reduce Fatigue
Our unique formula / helps you reduce / stress-induced fatigue / for heavy work loads.
Smarter / Decisions
Improve your short / term memory and / retain important / information / for longer.
Higher / Sustained / Energy
No crashes to give / you sustained energy / throughout the day

**Layout**

Title and subtitle top-left with a left-aligned intro paragraph beneath in a left column (about 4% to 30% width). Four white rounded cards are staggered across the centre and right, overlapping a tall product photo of a hand holding a can in the middle. Left cards are left-aligned; right cards are right-aligned. No footer or page number.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a hand holding a tall slim yellow-orange can labelled 'neutonic PRODUCTIVITY DRINK', tilted slightly clockwise, with a colourful iridescent illustrated eye (white, yellow, purple, pink, blue swirls) in the centre and small print 'FUEL YOUR FOCUS', ingredient list, 'ORANGE SUNRISE', 'ZERO SUGAR', 'FOOD SUPPLEMENT 330ML' near the bottom. The hand enters from the bottom edge, with fingers wrapping around the right side of the can. It is a cutout on the grey background, lit softly and evenly, with warm skin tones and golden yellow. Four white rounded-rectangle cards (large corner radius) sit behind or partly beside the can, which overlaps the Improved Attention, Smarter Decisions, Reduce Fatigue and Higher Sustained Energy cards. Background is a subtle grey pixel-block texture.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EBEBEB pixel-block mosaic texture with slightly lighter and darker squares. Light grey mosaic background with slightly varied square tiles. Title in near-black #221F20 bold geometric sans (Kumbh Sans Bold), about 5.4% of slide height. Subtitle in Kumbh Sans Regular #221F20, about 3.2%. Intro paragraph in Kumbh Sans Light, black, about 3.2%. Card headings in bold high-contrast serif (Libre Caslon Text Bold) #3B3636, about 4.4%. Card body in Kumbh Sans Regular #3B3636, about 2.7%. White card fills with large rounded corners, no visible shadow. Accent colours come from the yellow can.

**Layout**

Title and subtitle top-left at the left margin, with a full-width intro paragraph beneath. A large white rounded card on the left (about 75% wide) holds the heading '2026 Launch Plan' and five equal columns, each with product image, centered quarter/name and description. A narrow 'Beyond 2026' column of centered bold text sits on the right on the grey background. No footer or page number.

**Visuals**

Five product images in the white card, one per column. 1) Toothpicks: three tall slim tubes (green with spearmint, yellow/orange-red with peach, blue with cool mint) labelled 'neutonic NOOTROPICKS' with 'SQUEEZE TO OPEN' and an eye illustration, plus a thin wooden toothpick leaning at the right. 2) Focus Pouches: a round white tin seen from above at an angle, with a colourful swirling psychedelic eye graphic (teal pupil) on the lid and 'FOCUS POUCHES' text around it, a small white sachet lying at lower left. 3) Flavour Extensions: three slim cans in purple/green, red/blue/teal and green gradients, each with 'neutonic PRODUCTIVITY DRINK', a psychedelic eye, and 'FUEL YOUR FOCUS'. 4) Smart Creatine: a yellow square box with 'neutonic SMART CREATINE', a colourful swirly eye with a black pupil, and a peach-orange gradient at the bottom with 'PEACH MANGO ZERO SUGAR'. 5) Creatine RTD: a plain silver blank aluminium can, with no label. The background is a subtle grey tiled pattern of slightly different light-grey squares.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EDEDED with a subtle tiled pattern of slightly lighter and darker grey squares. Light grey patchwork background with a white rounded card. Titles and body text are near-black charcoal #221F20, with column text in black #000000. The typeface is Kumbh Sans, a geometric sans-serif, bold for the title and headings and regular for the subtitle and descriptions. The title is about 5.4% of slide height, the subtitle 3.2%, the intro paragraph 2.5%, and the column text 2.0 to 2.2%. The card has a large corner radius of about 2% and no visible shadow.

### Slide 12

**Text**

Launching With Impact
Previous Campaigns
We know how to command attention. Every new flavor or
product drop comes with a fun, high-impact campaign that
grabs attention. We’ve built a reputation for rolling out creative,
culturally relevant launches that cut through the clutter and
reach the masses.
Previous Campaigns
Americana USA Launch
The Matrix Capsules Launch

**Layout**

Two-part slide: left column holds title, subtitle, a five-line paragraph and a white rounded card with two campaign images and captions; right side has one tall photo filling most of the height. Left-aligned text with about 4% left margin; no footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Right: a tall photo of two bearded men in yellow-orange hazmat suits with green rubber gloves and respirator masks with pink filters, standing in a desert in front of a beige RV. Front man (center-left) wears a mask pushed up on his head and holds a can in his gloved hand; the man behind wears a full respirator and carries a silver metal briefcase. Warm golden-orange hazy light, sandy ground, yellow sky. Boxed with square corners. Inside the white card: left photo of a cowboy-dressed man standing on the roof of a pickup truck loaded with stacked cans, drinking from a can and holding an American flag, with a wooden Western building and a cloudy blue sky behind. Right photo is a Matrix-style two-part image: on the left a man in sunglasses and black clothes sitting in a black armchair holding a can under green falling code on a dark background; on the right two men in sunglasses and dark coats with hands outstretched toward the viewer, red capsules floating in the dark green-black scene.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EBEBEB with subtle lighter and darker square tile pattern. Light grey background with faint square tiles of slightly different greys. Title in near-black #221F20 bold geometric sans (Kumbh Sans Bold) about 5.4% of slide height; subtitle regular weight about 3.2%; body black regular about 2.7%. White card with large rounded corners (about 2% radius), no visible shadow. Card heading and captions bold.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A launch slide pairs a film-style narrative with a specific setting and visual treatment, showing how a product drop can be framed as a campaign.

**Text**

Breaking Blue Raspberry
Launching with Impact
For our Blue Raspberry launch, we drew on a cult-inspired narrative influenced by Breaking Bad. Shot in the California desert, the campaign followed Chris and James as they “cooked up” the purest blue on the market. Set around an RV and a makeshift lab, the shoot delivered cinematic, high-focus visuals.

**Layout**

Title, subtitle and a four-line paragraph are left-aligned in the top-left with a margin of about 4% from the left edge. Below them a wide white rounded panel holds three photos side by side, and a taller white panel on the right holds a fourth photo that extends higher than the others. No footer or page number.

**Visuals**

Four photographs in warm, golden, cinematic tones. 1) Two figures in yellow hazmat suits with green gloves and pink respirators, seen from behind in profile, walking in the desert and each holding a blue can. A white RV is parked in the background and the sky is dusty peach. 2) Close-up overhead shot of a light-blue can with a colourful psychedelic eye graphic (pink iris, black pupil) and the text 'FUEL YOUR FOCUS', beside blue laboratory glassware filled with purple-blue liquid, on a cream surface. 3) Two men in yellow suits with pink respirators pushed up on their heads, sitting in the cab of a van; the bearded driver in the foreground looks to the right, the passenger behind him drinks from a can; desert hills show through the window. 4) Tall portrait photo of a makeshift RV lab interior in orange-brown tones: a counter with flasks and beakers, one holding blue liquid, a green-gloved hand on the counter, a figure in a yellow suit with a pink respirator at the upper left, notes pinned to the wall, and an eye artwork. The three photos on the left are butted together with no gaps in one white panel with rounded corners. The fourth photo sits in a separate white rounded panel on the right, which merges with the first panel. A faint tiled grey texture covers the whole slide background.

**Design**

Background: Very light grey #EBEBEB with a faint tiled, lighter-to-darker pattern (a 10 by 6 pixel image stretched across the slide). Light grey, subtly tiled background. Title in black bold geometric sans-serif (Kumbh Sans Bold), about 5.4% of slide height. Subtitle in dark charcoal #221F20, regular weight, about 3.2%. Body in black Kumbh Sans Medium, about 2.7%, with comfortable line spacing. White panels have rounded corners of about 1.5% of slide width. No shadows or gradients.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

### Slide 14

**Text**

Strawberry Lemonade Miami Vice
Launching with Impact
For our Strawberry Lemonade launch, we took a Miami Vice–inspired approach, with Chris and James investigating the burnout from bad energy drinks. Set against the Miami skyline, the campaign featured clean, cinematic visuals with pastel suits, an 80s convertible, and a culmination centred around the new Strawberry Lemonade flavour.

**Layout**

Title, subtitle and a four-line paragraph are left-aligned at the top left with a margin of about 4% of slide width. A white rounded panel along the bottom holds three adjoining photos in a row. A tall white rounded panel on the right holds a fourth, portrait photo and overlaps the bottom panel's right end.

**Visuals**

Four photographs, all in white rounded-corner panels. Photo 1 (bottom left): two men in sunglasses and pastel suits, one yellow and one pink, each holding a Neutonic can, standing in front of a white vintage car with a city skyline behind under a blue-green sky; detective badges hang on their chests. Photo 2 (bottom, second from left): flat-lay of two yellow and pink Neutonic cans with a pink-and-yellow eye graphic, two black aviator sunglasses, two detective badges and yellow tape on a pale mint upholstered car seat. Photo 3 (bottom, third): front view of a white Oldsmobile convertible with two men in sunglasses at the windshield and a Neutonic can on the hood, with downtown buildings behind. Photo 4 (right, portrait): a white vintage convertible parked on a rooftop car park in warm sunlight, a man in a pink suit standing by its open door, with a tall apartment tower on the left and a glass-tower skyline against a cloudy blue sky. The photos have a warm, cinematic tone. The background is a faint light-grey pattern of squares.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #E8E8E8 with a faint tiled square pattern. Light grey background with a subtle tile pattern. Title in black bold geometric sans-serif (Kumbh Sans Bold) at about 5.4% of slide height. Subtitle in dark grey #221F20 regular weight at about 3.2%. Body in black medium weight at about 2.7%. The white panels have rounded corners of about 1.5% of slide width. No shadows.

On the right, a collage of tilted, rounded-corner Instagram story screenshots of yellow-green Neutonic cans with an eye logo, held in hands or placed on desks, cars, snowy scenery and cases of cans, with text overlays such as 'arrived!!', 'Sucker for new flavours', 'HYPE IS REAL', 'starting off a sleepy Monday the correct way', and '10/10'. Cost Breakdown and Margin Potential
Two years into the business, margins have improved materially through economies of scale,
infrastructure upgrades, and partnerships with larger manufacturers. Further upside remains,
with initiatives underway this year to reduce costs across the range through continued scale
efficiencies, formulation optimisation, and new manufacturing partners.

**Layout**

Left half holds a left-aligned title, subtitle, intro paragraph and a three-column table with a logo in the first column of each row. Top right has two white stat cards side by side. Below them, two tall portrait images sit side by side on the right, running nearly to the bottom edge.

**Visuals**

Table with a black header row and columns Retailer, Go Live Date, Store Count. Rows, each with a retailer logo in column 1: Tropicana Wholesale (red shield icon with black outlined wordmark) - LIVE - 750+ Gyms; Ocado (purple wordmark with swirl icon) - LIVE - Online; Booths (black small-caps wordmark) - LIVE - 26; Morrisons (green wordmark with yellow sunburst, Since 1899) - LIVE - 500; mfg motor fuel group (red italic logo) - Start Feb - 1250+ Petrol Stations; Sainsbury's (orange wordmark) - Mid Feb - 250+; Tesco (red wordmark over blue dashed stripes) - In Discussion - TBC. Two white rounded stat cards at top right. Left image is a Morrisons promotional graphic on a pale grey-white background: a green Morrisons shopping basket full of Neutonic cans with colourful eye artwork in blue, yellow, pink; caption text above in black serif 'Neutonic is now available in 500 Morrisons stores.'; at the bottom 'neutonic x Morrisons' with the green Morrisons logo and yellow sunburst. Right image is a photo of an in-store cardboard display stand in a supermarket aisle: orange and white stand headed 'FUEL + FOCUS DUO' with Grenade and Neutonic branding, shelves of blue Grenade protein bars labelled HIGH PROTEIN and LOW SUGAR, and Neutonic cans on the lower shelves, on a tiled floor with neighbouring shelves of products at left. The photo is lit by bright store lighting, with orange, white and blue dominant.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #E8E8E8 with faint lighter and darker rectangular tile pattern. Light grey background with subtle lighter and darker rectangular tiles. Title in dark charcoal #221F20 bold geometric sans (Kumbh Sans Bold), about 5.4% of slide height. Subtitle and body in black Kumbh Sans Regular, about 3.2% and 2.6%. Table header black #000000 fill with white bold text; table body text bold black with no row lines. Stat cards are white with slightly rounded corners and no visible shadow.

**Layout**

Left column holds the title, subtitle, intro paragraph, a three-column table with logos in the first column, and a white rounded panel of other retailer logos. Right side has two white stat cards at top and two side-by-side photos below. Title is top-left; text is left-aligned with a margin of about 4%. No footer or page number.

**Visuals**

Retailer logos in the table's first column: Central Market (green script, H-E-B small beneath), Gelson's (black script), The Vitamin Shoppe (navy V mark with navy caps text), GNC (red bold letters), Walgreens (red script). The white panel contains Kroger (blue wordmark with swoosh), Target (red bullseye with red wordmark), H-E-B (red oval with white letters), Whole Foods (green wordmark), Publix (green wordmark), Erewhon (black wide pixel-style caps). Two photos side by side. Left photo: a store display with a wooden table and black shelving unit stacked with rows of colourful canned drinks (pink, yellow, blue, green, orange, silver) with eye graphics, under a red sign reading 'SEWAR' in cream letters. Right photo: top-down view of a metal shopping cart filled with the same colourful eye-print cans, with two hands gripping the white handle with green ends at the bottom, on a grey concrete-textured background. A green circular Central Market 'CM' stamp logo sits at the top left of the photo.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EBEBEB with a subtle pattern of lighter and darker square tiles. Light grey background with faint lighter square tiles. Title in near-black #221F20 Kumbh Sans Bold, about 5.4% of slide height. Subtitle and body in black Kumbh Sans Regular, geometric sans-serif. Table header is a black bar with white bold text. Table cells have no fills or rules. Stat cards and logo panel are white with small rounded corners, no visible shadows.

### Slide 19

**Text**

Financial Projections
Driving Scalable Growth
Our projections are anchored in recent performance, with revenue doubling over the last 12 months. Ongoing strength across DTC and Amazon provides a solid foundation, while retail expansion and international growth in markets such as Australia and the UAE add meaningful upside.
The increase from 2025 to 26 is primarily driven by the scale of retail rollouts already secured and currently underway, which act as the key catalyst for the next phase of growth.
Revenue Projections
$50m
$24m
$8m
$4m
$900k
2023

2025
2026
2027

**Layout**

Left column holds the title, subtitle and two body paragraphs, all left-aligned at about 4% margin. A large white rounded card occupies the right half with a chart title and a bar chart inside. No footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Bar chart image in a white rounded card, titled 'Revenue Projections'. Five vertical bars with year labels 2023 to 2027 on the x-axis. Left y-axis is a bracket-style line with about 9 tick marks and no numeric labels. Values are printed above bars: 2023 $900k (very thin black bar), 2024 $4m (short black bar), 2025 $8m (black bar), 2026 $24m (teal bar), 2027 $50m (tallest teal bar). A vertical black dashed line separates 2025 from 2026, marking actuals (black bars) from projections (teal bars). No legend, no gridlines.

Body Kumbh Sans Light about 2.7% of height in black. Card is white with large corner radius (about 3% of slide width), no visible shadow. Chart accents are black #000000 and teal #00B5CC.

### Slide 20

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: the investment case is split by 14 pages of campaign, review and retail material, the funding ask is not stated until slide 20, and several slides carry very small text (3, 33, 39, 21) that is hard to read in a pitch.

**Text**

January 2026 Funding Round
Invest Now and Grow with Us
Neutonic is raising $3 million at a $60 million valuation, with $1.25 million already committed. This round represents our
final friends & family raise before moving to institutional investors in Q4 2026. We are opening the remaining $1.75
million to a small group of strategic partners who want to participate ahead of institutional pricing, based on the strong
growth and traction the brand has achieved to date.

Below are two white rounded cards: a wide left card (about 63% wide) containing a black-header table and a left-aligned heading, and a narrower right card with a two-column list. A rounded outlined callout straddles the bottom edge of the left card, and a small right-aligned footnote sits at the bottom right. No slide number.

**Visuals**

Left card: white rounded rectangle holding a 4-column, 5-row table with a black header row (white bold text) and four white body rows separated by thin black grid lines and a black outer border. Rows: $25,000 (Min Cheque Size) / $50k / 2x / $83,333; $50,000 / $100k / 2x / $166,667; 100,000 / $200k / 2x / $333,333; 250,000 / $500k / 2x / $833,333. Below the table a white rounded-rectangle callout with a thin black outline overlaps the card's bottom edge. Right card: white rounded rectangle listing six funding rounds, with the 'Jan 26 Friends and Family' row highlighted by a thin black rectangular outline spanning the card width. No images or icons.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EBEBEB with a faint patchwork of lighter and darker grey squares. Light grey background (#EBEBEB) with a subtle pale patchwork of lighter and darker grey squares. Title black #000000 in Kumbh Sans Bold, about 5.2% of slide height. Subtitle dark #221F20 Kumbh Sans Regular about 3.2%. Eight portrait cards in two rows of four, each a cream-bordered white card with a 4:5 photo and a serif name caption below. Row 1: Alan Barratt, a smiling man with short dark hair in a white collared shirt against a grey patterned background, with an orange GRENADE logo. Codie Sanchez, a woman with long wavy brown hair in a black top, outdoors with green foliage, with a black circular CT logo. Row 2: Daniel Priestley, a man in a dark shirt standing in an office, with a blue Dent logo and a score logo. Mike Thurston, a man in a white t-shirt holding out a neutonic can toward the camera, with an orange THRST logo. Hari Ravichandran, a bearded man with glasses in a blue shirt, with a black Aura logo and a jump ventures logo. Chris Donnelly, a man in a black t-shirt sitting in a green chair in front of a bookshelf, with a small blue The Creator Accelerator logo.

Background: Light grey #EDEDED with subtle square tiles of slightly different grey. Light grey tiled background with subtle lighter and darker squares. Cards have a cream #FAF3E3 border with a soft shadow and a white inner mat. Names are in a condensed serif font, dark grey #333333.

Q4 Series A Raise at $100+m
Invest Now and Grow with Us
Neutonic is now generating over $1 million in monthly revenue, with
clear visibility to $2 million per month and beyond from summer
onwards as retail expansion, increased distribution, and new product
launches come online. At a $2 million monthly run rate, this places
the business at $24 million+ in annualised revenue, which, applying a
conservative 4-5× revenue multiple, supports a $100 million+
valuation as the brand continues to scale.

**Visuals**

A bar chart in a white rounded card titled 'Forecasted Monthly Scale'. Y-axis labels are $500k, $1m, $1.5m and $2m, with light grey horizontal gridlines. X-axis categories are Nov 25, Dec 25, Jan 26, Feb 26, Mar 26, Apr 26, May 26, June 26 and July 26. Nine bars. The first two (Nov 25, Dec 25) are green at about $1m and $1.08m. The remaining seven are black: Jan 26 about $1.03m, Feb 26 about $1.2m, Mar 26 about $1.7m, Apr 26 about $1.38m, May 26 about $1.58m, June 26 about $1.67m, July 26 about $2.1m. A red upward-sloping trend line runs from about $0.8m at the left to about $2m at the right. The chart is a raster image, with Arial-like axis labels. No legend. Two white rounded rectangle cards on the right have no icons or logos.

Background: Light grey #EBEBEB with a subtle faint tiled gradient texture. Light grey background with a subtle faint tiled or gradient texture (#E8E8E8 to #F2F2F2). ### Slide 23

**Text**

Investment Objectives
Scaling Success
Since launching in 2023, Neutonic has delivered strong growth, generating over $13 million in revenue and building a loyal global customer base. In 2024, momentum accelerated through new sell-out product launches and expanded sales channels. In 2025, the focus shifted to expanding channels and international markets, while building the infrastructure needed to support retail at scale.
With 2026 underway and a strong pipeline of retail launches, this year marks the step change for Neutonic, driving retail growth, mass awareness, and category leadership.
Key Investment Objectives
Continue to Grow the Team
Expand the team across growth, retail, and distribution, while optimising existing web, subscription, and Amazon channels to drive stronger returns from proven revenue streams.
Retail Expansion UK and USA
Fund the marketing, activation, and execution required to launch and scale retail in both markets simultaneously, driving strong in-store performance and repeat purchase.
Build a Global Brand
Invest in brand, partnerships, and marketing to establish Neutonic as a leading global nootropic beverage brand and drive mass awareness.

**Visuals**

Background: Light grey pixel-mosaic pattern of subtle squares, about #E9E9E9, with tones from #DEDEDE to #F2F2F2. Light grey mosaic background (#E9E9E9) with subtle lighter and darker blocks. Titles and headings in black #000000, bold geometric sans-serif (Kumbh Sans Bold). Subtitle in dark charcoal #221F20, regular. Left body text is Kumbh Sans Regular, black, about 2.7% of slide height with generous line spacing. Card text uses Kumbh Sans Light, about 2.5% of height, for descriptions, and bold for sub-headings. The card is white (#FFFFFF) with a roughly 6% radius, no visible shadow or outline. Title is about 5.2% of slide height.

### Slide 24

**Text**

The market for Neutonic is ripe, the time is now.
Market is set to grow rapidly from $14bn to $24bn in 7 years, continuing growth trend of +$4bn 2021-2023
Current and forecast Nootropic market growth
Nootropic market growth driven by:
- Accelerated growth in APAC (+15.7% CAGR) and Europe (+14.9% CAGR)
- Growing health and wellbeing trend, and awareness of cognitive health supplement benefits
- Increased preference for naturally derived nootropic ingredients
- Consumers increasingly feeling depleted or distracted, and are searching for supplements that can help them recalibrate (28% US employees report feelings of burnout)
- Growing adoption of ‘smart drugs’ amongst students to support focus and learning stamina

### Slide 25

**Text**

The Future of Neutonic
Positioned for Strategic Growth and Acquisition
The future of Neutonic is focused on scalable, strategic
growth. With new product lines, expanded territories, and
a strong retail focus, the brand is positioned for consistent
year-on-year growth. These initiatives will increase brand
value and position Neutonic as a highly attractive
acquisition target for external investors.
Recent Relevant Acquisitions/Mergers
Majority stake acquired from Nutrabolt at
~$500m valuation. 2025.
Majority stake acquired from KDP at ~$1b
valuation. 2025.
Acquired by Unilever for ~$250m
Acquired by Pepsico for ~$2bn
Acquired by CongoBrands for ~$1.8bn
Who Could Buy Neutonic In The Future?
Nutrabolt, valued at $2.2 billion, is
behind C4, Cellucor, and Xtend and has
entered the nootropic market with
select C4 products. Their recent $90
million partial acquisition of Bloom
Nutrition highlights a shift toward health
and wellness trends, leveraging social
media creators.
Monster Beverage Corporation is
diversifying into health-focused,
functional beverages. Neutonic aligns
with this strategy, offering an
opportunity to tap into growing
demand, leverage its social media
presence, and scale the brand
globally.
Neutonic is on a clear path toward a highly lucrative exit.

**Layout**

Two-part layout. Left column holds the title, subtitle and intro paragraph at top, with a white rounded card below listing five acquisitions as logo plus text rows. Right side has a large white rounded card with a heading, two text columns under Nutrabolt and Monster logos, and a grid of six buyer logos in two rows. A bold closing sentence sits below the right card. No footer or page number.

**Visuals**

Logos. Left card, in rows: Bloom (dark green wordmark with leaf), Ghost (black graffiti-style wordmark with ghost icon), ONNIT (black italic wordmark), poppi (hot pink script wordmark), Alani Nu (teal script wordmark). Right card: nutrabolt (green and grey wordmark with N icon), Monster Energy (green claw-mark M icon plus black Monster Energy wordmark). Lower grid, row 1: The Coca-Cola Company (black script), PepsiCo (globe icon with blue PEPSICO), THEHUTGROUP (black and grey spaced caps). Row 2: Keurig Dr Pepper (dark grey wordmark with pink and green icon), Red Bull (red wordmark with red bulls and yellow sun), Unilever (blue U emblem with blue script wordmark), AB InBev (yellow icon with black wordmark). The background has a subtle light grey checker/tile texture.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EBEBEB with subtle lighter and darker tile patchwork. Light grey subtle patchwork background. Title is black bold sans-serif (Kumbh Sans Bold), about 5.2% of slide height. Subtitle is dark charcoal #221F20 regular. Body text is black Kumbh Sans Light and Regular. Card headings are bold black. White cards have large corner radii (about 3% of slide height), no visible shadow or borders. Closing line is bold black.

### Slide 26

**Text**

Thank You!
neutonic

**Layout**

Closing slide inside a large rounded light-grey card with a small margin. The title 'Thank You!' sits top-left; the neutonic wordmark sits bottom-left; the right ~60% holds a render of five cans on a grey stepped ledge sloping downward to the right.

**Visuals**

A 3D product render of five neutonic productivity drink cans standing in a row on a grey angular ledge that slopes down to the right, seen from a low angle. Each can has a large surreal eye illustration in a swirling iridescent yellow, pink, purple and blue blob. Cans left to right: pink (pink iris, STRAWBERRY LEMONADE), white (teal iris, TROPICAL ICE), yellow-green top with green lower half (green iris, WILD CITRUS), light blue (red iris, BLUE RASPBERRY), orange (amber iris, ORANGE SUNRISE). Each has the neutonic wordmark in black serif, PRODUCTIVITY DRINK, FUEL YOUR FOCUS, ingredient list (COGNIZIN, RHODIOLA ROSEA, PANAX GINSENG, L-THEANINE, 120MG NATURAL CAFFEINE), a black flavour pill, ZERO SUGAR badge, FOOD SUPPLEMENT 330ML. Soft even lighting on a pale grey backdrop. A darker grey rectangle block sits behind the left end of the ledge. A bottom-left neutonic wordmark logo in black heavy serif.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #E9E9EB card on a paler pixel-block grey surround #EFEFEF. Soft light-grey scheme with a rounded card (radius about 2% of width) on a slightly lighter, subtly blocky pale-grey surround. Title in near-black #221F20 bold geometric sans (Kumbh Sans Bold) at about 6.4% of slide height. Product cans give the colour accents: yellow #F2CF45, pink #E98CA3, blue #8CCBEA, green #A9CF7F, orange #F2A63C. No shadows beyond those in the render.

### Slide 28

**Text**

Product Lineup
Since launching with our Productivity Drinks in 2023, we have significantly expanded and now have 4 amazing products
types, each has a different use case and potential customer.
PRODUCTIVITY DRINK
Our Original, Powerful, and
Delicious Caffeinated Blend
BRAIN CAPSULES
Our Ultimate Blend,
in Daily Pill Form
FOCUS BLENDS
Our Uncaffeinated Stick Packs,
Perfect For Anytime Of Day
CREATINE STICKS
Our Creapure® Creatine Sticks,
Designed to Fuel Your Performance

**Layout**

Title at top left with a two-line intro paragraph beneath, both left-aligned with a margin of about 4%. Below, four equal columns, each with a centred bold uppercase heading, a two-line centred description and a product image beneath. No footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Four product photographs on a light grey background, all neutonic-branded packaging with a psychedelic rainbow eye graphic. Column 1: a row of five slim cans (blue, orange/pink, white in front, yellow, green), the white centre can largest, overlapping, shown in soft studio lighting. Column 2: a black bottle with a white/iridescent label showing an eye, tilted, with scattered dark red capsules floating around it. Column 3: yellow and pink stick-pack boxes and individual sachets (yellow and pink) floating at angles, with an open display box of sticks, soft shadows below. Column 4: three tall white sachets with yellow, blue and red tops standing above three white creatine boxes (the centre one largest) with strawberry, unflavoured and pineapple colour labels. Some edges are cropped off by the next image.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EBEBEB with faint lighter and darker tiled squares. Light grey background with faint subtle tiled lighter and darker squares. Title in near-black #221F20 bold geometric sans-serif (Kumbh Sans Bold), about 5.4% of slide height. Intro text and descriptions in black Kumbh Sans Regular, about 3% and 2.5% of height. Column headings bold uppercase #221F20 at about 3.2%. No lines, shadows or gradients on the shapes, only soft shadows in the product photos.

### Slide 32

**Text**

Creatine Sachets
Our Creatine Sachets deliver 5g of premium Creapure®
creatine monohydrate, the world’s most trusted and
rigorously tested form of creatine. Known for its purity,
safety and effectiveness, it’s designed to support
performance and high intensity exercise. Neutonic
Creatine Sachets offer a simple, clean way to fuel your
training.
Ingredient Profile
5G
Creapure
Caffeine-Free
Zero Sugar
Natural Sweeteners
Natural Flavours

**Layout**

Two-column layout. Left column holds the title at the top, a paragraph, an 'Ingredient Profile' subheading, a white rounded card with the Creapure logo and '5G Creapure', and a 2x2 grid of pill outlines. Right half is a product photo of three sachets standing in three boxes. No footer or page number.

**Visuals**

Right side: product photograph on a light grey studio background. Three Neutonic creatine sachets (yellow-tipped Pineapple, grey-tipped Unflavoured, red-tipped Strawberry Lime) stand upright, fanned out, in the openings of three white Neutonic boxes. Each sachet shows a 'neutonic' wordmark and a psychedelic multicoloured eye graphic (yellow, blue and red pupils respectively). The boxes are in the foreground, with the large centre box showing a blue-pupilled eye, the left box a red-pupilled eye and the right box a yellow-pupilled eye. The boxes are slightly pixelated/low resolution, with a soft shadow beneath. Left: Creapure logo (black parallelogram-outline badge with the italic 'Creapure' wordmark, three black bars and '25PO15') inside a white rounded card. Four black-outlined pill shapes with white or transparent fill.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EBEBEB with subtle lighter and darker square tonal patches. Soft light grey background with subtle faint square tonal blocks. Title in near-black #221F20 bold geometric sans (Kumbh Sans Bold), about 5.4% of slide height. Body in Kumbh Sans Light black, about 3% of slide height, line spacing about 3.5%. Subheading bold #221F20. Pills have 1.5px black outline, fully rounded ends, no fill. White card with about 2% corner radius, no shadow.

### Slide 33

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Each ingredient gets a consistent structure of a short description followed by study findings, which keeps dense scientific detail easy to scan.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or cut the micro-text on slides 33 and 39, make the market map on 35 readable with clear axis placement and a labelled claim, and remove duplicate slides (26 and 42, 28 and 8).

**Text**

Product Detail
The Science Between Our Nootropic Blend
Cognizin
A patented form of citicoline that has been extensively clinically studied in healthy adults. Aids in the production of acetylcholine, a key neurotransmitter, while supporting phospholipid synthesis and brain metabolism.
Study 1: Adolescent males taking 500mg of Cognizin® exhibited improved attention and a significant increase in psychomotor speed compared to placebo
Study 2: Women taking 500mg of Cognizin® experienced fewer omission and commission errors compared to placebo.
THE UNIVERSITY OF UTAH
Rhodiola Rosea
Rhodiola rosea has been observed in numerous studies to alleviate fatigue caused by psychological stress.
Study 1: 120 individuals suffering from burnout given a daily dose of 400mg for 12 weeks demonstrated significant improvement for all stress related symptoms.
Study 2: Supplementation of Rhodiola rosea had a significant effect on reducing stress induced fatigue in physicians
MEDICAL UNIVERSITY OF VIENNA
L-Theanine
L-Theanine is thought to enhance alpha-wave activity in the brain, which is associated with creativity, focus and attention.
Study 1: Healthy adults taking 200mg of L-theanine daily for 4 weeks had improve cognitive function and reduced stress related symptoms
Study 2: Visual reaction time was significantly improved by L-theanine supplementation in healthy adults
UNIVERSITY OF PERADENIYA
Panax Ginseng
Panax ginseng contains ginsenosides, which have been shown to influence various neurotransmitter systems in the brain.
Study 1: Supplementation of 200mg of Panax ginseng significantly improve performance and feelings of mental fatigue in healthy adults
Study 2: The study's results showed that 200mg of Panax ginseng enhanced performances on a calculation task and reduced the increase in mental fatigue experienced by participants during the later stages of a demanding mental task.
Northumbria University NEWCASTLE

**Layout**

Title and subtitle top-left aligned at about 4% left margin. Below them a 2x2 grid of four white rounded cards; in each card a left column holds a logo/image and bold serif name, and a right column holds a description paragraph followed by two Study entries. Empty band at the bottom; no footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Four white rounded-rectangle cards. Cognizin card: blue 'Cognizin' wordmark logo with red dots over the 'i', and University of Utah logo (red block U with 'THE UNIVERSITY OF UTAH' in black serif). Rhodiola card: photo of a green Rhodiola rosea plant with yellow flowers (cut-out), and Medical University of Vienna logo (round navy emblem with 'MEDICAL UNIVERSITY OF VIENNA'). L-Theanine card: cut-out photo of green tea leaves and dried tea, and University of Peradeniya logo (round red/gold seal with Sinhala and Tamil text and 'UNIVERSITY OF PERADENIYA'). Panax Ginseng card: cut-out photo of a ginseng root, and Northumbria University Newcastle logo (black shield crest with 'Northumbria University NEWCASTLE'). 'Study 1:'/'Study 2:' labels are blue underlined bold links.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #E8E8E8 with subtle tiled squares in lighter and darker grey. Light grey background with faint lighter and darker square tiling. Title bold near-black #221F20 sans (Kumbh Sans Bold, about 5.4% of slide height); subtitle Kumbh Sans Regular about 3.2%. Card headings Libre Caslon Text Bold serif about 3.2%, #221F20. Body Kumbh Sans Light about 2% in #221F20; Study labels Kumbh Sans Bold blue #0000FF underlined. Cards white with large corner radius (about 5% of height), no visible stroke.

**Layout**

Large rounded light-grey panel inset from the slide edges. Title and subtitle top-left. A four-column grid of product photos with bold labels beneath, then two rows of white rounded price cards (UK row, USA row) with pill labels on the left. Neutonic wordmark bottom-left.

**Visuals**

Four product photos on the light-grey background: (1) a 12-can tray of Neutonic Productivity Drink cans, silver cans with a rainbow-swirled eye graphic, tray printed '12 CANS neutonic FUEL YOUR FOCUS'; (2) a dark brown Brain Capsules bottle with a colourful eye label, pixelated; (3) an orange/yellow Neutonic Focus Blend box with an eye graphic, 'CAFFEINE FREE', 'PEACH MANGO', 'ZERO SUGAR'; (4) a white and red Creatine Sticks box with a colourful eye graphic, pixelated. Eight white rounded-corner price cards in a 4x2 grid. Two outlined pill shapes (black 1px outline, no fill) labelled UK and USA. Black 'neutonic' serif wordmark logo bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EBEBEB with a lighter rounded inner panel #E8E8EA. Light grey #EBEBEB background with a slightly lighter rounded panel (#E8E8EA) and faint tile pattern at the edges. Title bold near-black #221F20, Kumbh Sans-style geometric sans-serif, about 5.4% of slide height; subtitle regular about 3.2%. Prices extra-bold black #000000 at about 6.9% height; labels and per-unit lines regular black at about 2.7%; subscription lines bold at about 2.5%. White cards with about 2% corner radius, no shadow.

### Slide 35

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A simple two-axis market map places competitors by nootropic dosage and B-vitamin content, making the positioning visible at a glance.
- Review: The market map on slide 35 has an empty quadrant, uneven product placement and axis labels that sit awkwardly, and the thank-you and appendix repeat slides (26, 42, 28, 8).

**Text**

Market Comparison
Market Map
The market is currently divided
into caffeinated energy drinks that
lack nootropic benefits, drinks
marketed as nootropics with
minimal cognitive impact, and
those with higher doses of
nootropics but limited in other
areas, such as caffeine or vitamins.
100% RI - B Vitamins
High Nootropic Dosage
No/Low Nootropic Dosage
0% RI - B Vitamins

**Layout**

Title and subtitle top-left, left-aligned, with a paragraph of body text beneath in the left third. A large white rounded-rectangle panel fills the right two-thirds, holding a 2x2 market map with a vertical and a horizontal axis line crossing near its centre and can images placed in the quadrants. No footer or page number.

**Visuals**

A 2x2 market map inside a white rounded panel. A thin navy vertical line runs from about y 29% to y 82% at x about 63%, and a thin navy horizontal line runs from x about 37% to x about 90% at y about 53%. Axis labels: top '100% RI - B Vitamins', bottom '0% RI - B Vitamins', left 'No/Low Nootropic Dosage', right 'High Nootropic Dosage'. Product photos of cans on transparent or white backgrounds. Upper left quadrant (high vitamins, low dosage): a black NOCCO Focus can, a white and orange/purple XITE nootropic energy can, and a red/yellow C4 Smart Energy can. Upper right quadrant (high vitamins, high dosage): a black and blue Jocko GO Real Energy can, an orange Gorilla Mind can with a blue gorilla graphic, and a white neutonic Productivity Drink can with a colourful eye illustration. Lower left quadrant (0% vitamins, low dosage): a purple and black TENZING Natural Energy can, a green Perfect Ted Healthy Energy pear ginger can, and a white brite Natural Focus+ Energy pineapple mango can. Lower right quadrant is empty. Background is a subtle grey tiled pattern.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EBEBEB with a faint tiled square pattern in slightly lighter and darker greys. Light grey background with faint square tile variations. Title in dark charcoal #221F20 bold geometric sans-serif (Kumbh Sans Bold), about 5.4% of slide height. Subtitle in Kumbh Sans Regular about 3.2%. Body in Kumbh Sans Light black, about 3.2%. Map labels Kumbh Sans Light about 2.7%. Panel is white with large corner radius (about 5% of slide height) and no visible shadow. Axis lines are navy #1F3A6B, about 2px.

**Layout**

Title and subtitle top-left, left-aligned. Two stacked white rounded cards in the left 46% of the slide, each with a bold heading and a 3x3 grid of words. Four product photos stand in a row on the right half, bottoms aligned around 80% height. No footer or slide number.

**Visuals**

Four product shots on a light grey background. 1) A pixelated, blurred yellow-orange beverage can with a large psychedelic eye graphic (white eyeball, black pupil, rainbow swirl around it), mosaic-pixelated so the text is unreadable. 2) A black-capped dark bottle labelled 'neutonic' in a black serif wordmark, with a holographic rainbow eye (blue iris) in a circle, and the text 'BRAIN CAPSULES', supports list, '90 CAPSULES DIETARY SUPPLEMENT', on a pastel blue-to-yellow gradient label. 3) A narrow hot-pink stick pack, 'neutonic', a small eye graphic, 'FOCUS BLEND', 'WILD BERRY ZERO SUGAR'. 4) A narrow white and grey stick pack with a red bottom edge, 'neutonic', an eye graphic, 'CREATINE STICKS', 'STRAWBERRY LIME ZERO SUGAR', 'NET WT 0.26OZ (7.5g)'. The 'WE ARE NOT' words are struck through with a thin dark line. Soft shadows sit under the products.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #EBEBEB, a faint tiled pixel-mosaic texture. Very light grey #EBEBEB background with faint, subtle square tile variations. Title is bold, near-black #221F20, about 5.4% of slide height, in Kumbh Sans (geometric sans-serif). Subtitle and word lists are Kumbh Sans Regular at about 3.2%, in #221F20. Headings 'WE ARE' and 'WE ARE NOT' are Kumbh Sans Bold in capitals at about 3.2%. White cards with a large corner radius (about 2% of height), no visible stroke or shadow. 'WE ARE NOT' words have a strikethrough.

### Slide 38

**Text**

Customer Profile
Our Customers Need Neutonic
Our typical customer:
- Typically between the age of 15-40
- Living in or near big cities
- Striving to earn a good income
- Interested in self-improvement
- Listens to podcasts on success and growth
- Focused on improving their physical health
- Tech-savvy and uses social media heavily
- Follows trends, willing to try new things
Without Neutonic our customers:
Drink a redbull at work to focus
Drink a monster to work out
Drink a coffee before work
Takes a pro-plus to study
Currently consumers are taking Caffeine for different uses cases, and expecting
different results. To work, focus and be more productive, customers need a
proven cognitive enhancing nootropic and caffeine blend

**Layout**

Title and subtitle top-left. Two white rounded cards sit side by side in the middle (left: bullet list, right: centered statements), with a wide white rounded banner below containing a coffee bean image at left and a paragraph at right. A round eye graphic overlaps the gap between the two upper cards at the centre. No footer or page number.

**Visuals**

A glossy spherical eye illustration, centered between the two upper cards and overlapping both: a green iris with black pupil and white highlight, white eyeball, wrapped in swirling holographic paint-like bands of yellow, orange, pink, purple, blue and teal against a pale pink sphere. A small photo of five brown roasted coffee beans with a soft shadow on a white background, at the left of the bottom banner. Faint grey square tile pattern in the background.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #E8E8E8 with a subtle tiled pattern of slightly lighter and darker squares. Light grey background with subtle lighter and darker square tiles. Titles and subtitle in near-black charcoal #221F20, card text pure black #000000. Typeface Kumbh Sans, a geometric sans-serif: bold for title and card headings, regular for body. Title is about 5.4% of slide height, subtitle 3.2%, card headings 3.3%, list 2.35%, right-card statements 3.1%, banner text 2.7%. White cards have large corner radii (about 3% of slide height) with no visible shadow or border.

### Slide 42

**Text**

Thank You!
neutonic

**Layout**

Large rounded light-grey panel inset about 1.7% from the slide edges. Title 'Thank You!' sits top-left. The neutonic wordmark is bottom-left. A product photo of five cans on a grey stepped ledge fills the right and lower-right, cropped at the right edge of the panel.

**Visuals**

Photo of five neutonic productivity drink cans standing in a row on a descending grey ramp, viewed from a low angle. The cans step downward from left to right and shrink slightly with perspective. Flavours from left to right: pink Strawberry Lemonade, white Tropical Ice, green Wild Citrus, blue Blue Raspberry, orange Orange Sunrise. Each can has a black 'neutonic' wordmark with 'PRODUCTIVITY DRINK' under it. Each has a large psychedelic iridescent swirl around a single eye; the iris is pink, teal, green, crimson and amber respectively. Below the eye is 'FUEL YOUR FOCUS' with the ingredient list COGNIZIN, RHODIOLA ROSEA, PANAX GINSENG, L-THEANINE, 120MG NATURAL CAFFEINE, a black flavour pill, a ZERO SUGAR tag, and FOOD SUPPLEMENT 330ML. The cans' tops are yellow, white, yellow, light blue and orange. The ramp is light grey with soft shadows and its lit surface is light. A light grey rectangle (#D9D9D9) sits behind the ramp's left end. The lighting is soft and even in a studio. The neutonic wordmark at bottom-left is black, in a bold serif display face.

**Design**

Background: Light grey #E8E8EA with a slightly lighter rounded panel inset. Soft light-grey background (#E8E8EA) with a slightly lighter rounded panel (#EAEAEC) with large corner radius. Title in bold geometric sans (Kumbh Sans Bold), near-black #221F20, about 6.4% of slide height. Wordmark in black heavy serif. No shadows or gradients except those in the photo.
